censys@infosec.exchange at 2026-10-16T19:19:05.000Z ##

🚨 Rapid Response | BeyondTrust Remote Support/Privileged Remote Access Zero-Day (CVE-2026-102764) (CVSS 9.3) is a critical vulnerability.

The flaw allows unauthenticated attackers to perform file operations on affected appliances.
Censys currently detects 9,569 Internet-exposed hosts and 177,497 web properties running BeyondTrust Remote Support or Privileged Remote Access. These figures represent detected installations, not confirmed vulnerable or unpatched instances.

BeyondTrust has released patches for both products. Organizations should prioritize patching self-hosted appliances and investigate potential compromise, as patching does not remediate prior exploitation.

Full Censys ARC advisory, including affected versions, exploitation indicators, and remediation guidance: https://censys.com/advisory/cve-2026-102764/

1 repos

https://github.com/murrez/CVE-2026-89079

DarkWebInformer@infosec.exchange at 2026-10-16T18:21:28.000Z ##

🚨 PoC published for a critical unauthenticated WordPress Theme My Login account takeover vulnerability; CVE-2026-89079

PoC: https://github.com/murrez/CVE-2026-89079

The flaw affects WordPress multisite installations using Theme My Login with user-chosen registration passwords enabled. Under the required configuration, an unauthenticated attacker can abuse the lost-password flow to overwrite another user's password and take over the account.

CVSS: 9.8 Critical
Affected: Theme My Login ≤ 7.2.1
Fixed: Theme My Login ≥ 7.2.2

Exploitation is configuration-dependent and requires WordPress multisite with the tml_user_passwords network option enabled. The PoC includes target checking, verification, bulk scanning, and an exploit mode that validates successful account access.

##
secdb@infosec.exchange at 2026-10-16T13:09:31.000Z ##

🚨 PwnEscape (CVE-2026-99999) has been identified as a notable vulnerability.

In the Linux kernel, the following vulnerability has been resolved:

ipv4: validate checksum_start before completing checksum

If a packet with bad checksum metadata gets into the ipv4 stack,
skb_checksum_help can corrupt the network header and cause a bunch of
mischief.

This was discovered and reported by Paulos, and has been reporoduced
by others independently since.

We really shouldn't allow such packets in, but as a defence
in depth measure, let's also check before we complete the checksum.

A more complete validation at input is forthcoming, but needs more work.

ℹ️ Additional details, advisories and sightings on ZEN SecDB 👉 https://secdb.nttzen.cloud/cve/detail/CVE-2026-99999

#InfoSec #PwnEscape #Linux #Kernel #CVE202699999
#NTTDATA #ZENSecDB

##
Larvitz@burningboard.net at 2026-10-16T07:54:35.000Z ##

Heads up for fellow Linux admins: CVE-2026-99999 (dubbed "PwnEscape") is out, and it is a nasty one (CVSS 9.3).

Despite being called a KVM escape, the actual bug lives in the host IPv4 networking stack. Untrusted CHECKSUM_PARTIAL offload metadata from a guest can overwrite already-validated packet headers during ip_do_fragment(), leaking host memory or allowing a guest-to-host escape.

Mainline patches landed Oct 14, and stable backports are out. Check your hypervisors and reboot!

https://pwn.ai/blog/pwnescape

#Linux #Kernel #SysAdmin #InfoSec #KVM #ITSEC #CVE

censys@infosec.exchange at 2026-10-16T14:26:24.000Z ##

🚨 Rapid Response | Collabora Online Built-in CODE Server OS Command Injection (CVE-2026-110447) (CVSS 9.2) is a critical OS command injection vulnerability affecting the Collabora Online Built-in CODE Server app for Nextcloud. Exploitation in the wild has been reported, allowing unauthenticated attackers to execute operating system commands.
Censys currently detects 19,454 Internet-exposed hosts and 103,350 web properties with the app installed. These figures represent detected installations, not confirmed vulnerable instances.

Organizations should upgrade to version 26.4.303 or later, or remove the app. Disabling it is not sufficient.

Full Censys ARC advisory, including affected versions, exploitation indicators, and remediation guidance: https://censys.com/advisory/cve-2026-110447/

#CensysARC #Cybersecurity #VulnerabilityManagement #Nextcloud #InfoSec

www.google.com/search
microsoft cves october 2026 DWM
In the October 2026 Patch Tuesday release, Microsoft addressed several vulnerabilities in the Windows Desktop Window Manager (DWM) and its Core Library. These flaws are part of a record-breaking update cycle fixing over 1,400 vulnerabilities. Qualys +3

##
censys@infosec.exchange at 2026-10-15T14:42:19.000Z ##

🚨 Rapid Response: Ivanti Connect Secure Authentication Bypass (CVE-2026-104935)

Ivanti confirms active exploitation of a high-severity (CVSS 8.6) authentication bypass vulnerability in Ivanti Connect Secure that allows unauthenticated attackers to execute arbitrary commands. CISA added the flaw to its Known Exploited Vulnerabilities (KEV) catalog on October 13.

Censys currently observes 7,604 Internet-exposed hosts and 269,319 web properties running Ivanti Connect Secure, excluding honeypots. These are total product exposures, not confirmed vulnerable systems.

Full Censys ARC advisory, including affected versions, available patches, and remediation guidance: https://censys.com/advisory/cve-2026-104935/

This ties the life cycle of scheduling stats with mm. In account_mm_sched(), the scheduling stats are accessed by task->mm->sc_stat. However, a task m

harrysintonen@infosec.exchange at 2026-10-16T22:09:52.000Z ##

Ah it's weekend again: "... vulnerabilities can be exploited for Linux server LPE, container escape, and Android LPE."

CVE-2026-98260 (Timer)
This is a use-after-free caused by the interaction between an exec() failure in a thread other than the process leader and POSIX CPU timer cleanup.

CVE-2026-98256 (Signal)
This is a sigqueue use-after-free caused by a race between exec() in a nonleader thread and cleanup of a private pending signal queue.

CVE-2026-89754 (MM)
Retry state from the page table walker escapes into an upper level, causing an out-of-bounds write to the temporary result buffer used by mincore().

CVE-2026-80775 (Futex)
This is a use-after-free caused when two tasks that share an mm_struct through nested vfork() calls race to allocate the initial reference counter for FUTEX_PRIVATE_HASH.

CVE-2026-98385 (Scheduler)
This is a use-after-free caused when scheduler cache accounting continues to update per-CPU statistics belonging to a previously freed mm_struct. The vulnerable path is reached when CONFIG_SCHED_CACHE is enabled, scheduler cache is active at runtime, and the CPU topology contains multiple LLCs.

CVE-2026-97941 (SLAB)
This is a list corruption caused by a race between returning a freelist and removing a slab from the node partial list during SLUB object refill. The public trigger causes a kernel panic.

https://www.openwall.com/lists/oss-security/2026/10/16/36

updated 2026-09-25T18:17:34.240000

1 posts

A flaw was found in librsvg. When processing an SVG document containing nested XML inclusions (Xincludes) with duplicate entity declarations, a use-after-free error can occur. This vulnerability arises because the library incorrectly frees an XML entity that is still in use by the parser. An attacker could potentially exploit this to cause a denial of service or execute arbitrary code.

harrysintonen@infosec.exchange at 2026-10-16T22:09:52.000Z ##

Ah it's weekend again: "... vulnerabilities can be exploited for Linux server LPE, container escape, and Android LPE."

CVE-2026-98260 (Timer)
This is a use-after-free caused by the interaction between an exec() failure in a thread other than the process leader and POSIX CPU timer cleanup.

CVE-2026-98256 (Signal)
This is a sigqueue use-after-free caused by a race between exec() in a nonleader thread and cleanup of a private pending signal queue.

CVE-2026-89754 (MM)
Retry state from the page table walker escapes into an upper level, causing an out-of-bounds write to the temporary result buffer used by mincore().

CVE-2026-80775 (Futex)
This is a use-after-free caused when two tasks that share an mm_struct through nested vfork() calls race to allocate the initial reference counter for FUTEX_PRIVATE_HASH.

CVE-2026-98385 (Scheduler)
This is a use-after-free caused when scheduler cache accounting continues to update per-CPU statistics belonging to a previously freed mm_struct. The vulnerable path is reached when CONFIG_SCHED_CACHE is enabled, scheduler cache is active at runtime, and the CPU topology contains multiple LLCs.

CVE-2026-97941 (SLAB)
This is a list corruption caused by a race between returning a freelist and removing a slab from the node partial list during SLUB object refill. The public trigger causes a kernel panic.

https://www.openwall.com/lists/oss-security/2026/10/16/36

beyondmachines1@infosec.exchange at 2026-10-15T13:01:49.000Z ##

Critical Ghostscript Flaw Allows Remote Code Execution via PDF Uploads

Artifex Ghostscript versions 9.21 to 10.07.1 contain a critical heap buffer overflow (CVE-2026-39919) that allows unauthenticated remote code execution via crafted PDF uploads. The flaw impacts web platforms like WordPress that use Ghostscript for document processing and thumbnail generation.

**If your servers or websites (including WordPress) create previews or thumbnails from uploaded PDFs, update Ghostscript to version 10.08.0 (or your Linux vendor's patched package) ASAP, and rebuild any containers from updated images. If you can't patch immediately, turn off PDF processing in ImageMagick or stop accepting PDF uploads from anyone you don't trust.**

https://github.com/Nero22k/CVE-2025-41450_poc

marcel@waldvogel.family at 2026-10-16T03:05:44.000Z ##

@HonkHase
Es ist nicht das Datum selbst, sondern eine leichte Abwandlung.

Mit dem auf vielen Unix-Linuxsystemen standardnässig verfügbaren Uralt-Kommandozeilen-Taschenrechner lässt sich das Passwort für gestern mit folgenden zwri Eingabezeilen errechnen:

ibase=16
10152610 / 15

Die "10", "15" und "26" oben sind Monat, Tag und Jahr; `ibase=16` sagt, dass die als Hex- statt Dezimalsequenzen zu interpretieren seien. Das Passwort sind die letzten 5 Ziffern des Resultats.

https://ironhackers.es/en/danfoss-ak-sm-800a-cve-2025-41450/#the-code-of-the-day

CVE-2025-60492
(0 None)

EPSS: 0.00%

2 posts

N/A

sigdevel at 2026-10-17T14:12:07.664Z ##

Security Advisory: CVE-2025-60492 - Uncontrolled Recursion in GPAC/MP4Box

Processing a crafted MP4 file with specially constructed BIFS scene data causes unbounded recursion between BIFS decoder functions, exhausting the stack and crashing MP4Box.

Summary:
The `gf_bifs_dec_node` function in `bifs/field_decode.c` decodes MPEG-4 BIFS scene nodes by calling `BD_DecMFFieldList`, `gf_bifs_dec_field`, and `gf_bifs_dec_node_list`, which in turn call back into `gf_bifs_dec_node`. A crafted MP4 file with deeply or cyclically nested BIFS containers triggers this mutual recursion without a depth limit, consuming all available stack memory and crashing the process with a stack-overflow signal.

CWE:
CWE-674 - Uncontrolled Recursion

Affected Component:
```
bifs/field_decode.c:905
Function: gf_bifs_dec_node()
```

Affected Product:
MP4Box (GPAC Multimedia Open Source Project)

Affected Version:
2.5-DEV-rev1687-ge44a4e2b0-master; commit `e44a4e2b0d193566619ada71599e70255699da94`. Any codebase equivalent to this commit that has not applied the fix commit is affected.

Attack Conditions:
An attacker supplies a crafted MP4 file whose embedded BIFS scene data contains deeply nested or mutually referencing node containers. Local access is required; the victim must invoke `MP4Box -x3d <crafted_file>` or any equivalent BIFS scene export command that triggers the recursive decoder path.

Impact:
The uncontrolled recursion exhausts the process stack, resulting in an immediate crash and Denial of Service. No evidence of arbitrary code execution was observed; stack-exhaustion crashes via recursion do not provide an exploitable control-flow primitive.

Fix / mitigation status:
The issue was fixed in GPAC commit:
```
8e7b9a3a04331286b89d1803c65a2d6edc11b4c4
```
Users should update to a GPAC build containing this commit or later.

References:

- Issue: https://github.com/gpac/gpac/issues/3324
- PoC: https://github.com/sigdevel/pocs/blob/main/res/gpac/MP4Box/59/59_Anchor_Create_scenegraph_mpeg4_nodes_c_171
- Fix: https://github.com/gpac/gpac/commit/8e7b9a3a04331286b89d1803c65a2d6edc11b4c4

Credit
Alexander A. Shvedov (@sigdevel)

#fuzzing #infosec #security #afl++ #revers #cybersecurity #bugbounty #vulnerability #opensource #linux #cve #advisory #gpac

CVE-2025-60488
(0 None)

EPSS: 0.00%

sigdevel at 2026-10-17T14:10:22.887Z ##

Security Advisory: CVE-2025-60488 - NULL Pointer Dereference in GPAC/MP4Box

Processing a crafted MP4 file with a missing Sample Description box triggers a NULL pointer dereference in `dump_isom_saps`, causing MP4Box to crash.

Summary:
The `dump_isom_saps` function in `applications/mp4box/filedump.c` iterates over track metadata to dump SAP (Sync Access Point) information. When the input MP4 contains a corrupted track structure where the Sample Description box (`stsd`) is absent, the function accesses track metadata pointers without prior validation, dereferencing a near-NULL address (0x1c, 28-byte struct offset) and triggering SIGSEGV.

CWE:
CWE-476 - NULL Pointer Dereference

Affected Component:
```
applications/mp4box/filedump.c:1743
Function: dump_isom_saps()
```

Affected Product:
MP4Box (GPAC Multimedia Open Source Project)

Affected Version:
2.5-DEV-rev1672-gc9a31d0e1-master; commit `c9a31d0e1b649a30814c6d81c5461859ed63d075`. Any codebase equivalent to this commit that has not applied the fix commit is affected.

Attack Conditions:
An attacker supplies a crafted MP4 file whose track structure lacks a valid `stsd` box. Local access is required; the victim must invoke `MP4Box -dsap 1 <crafted_file>` or any equivalent SAP analysis command that exercises the SAP dump path.

Impact:
The near-NULL pointer dereference (READ at address 0x00000000001c) causes an immediate process crash, resulting in Denial of Service. No evidence of arbitrary code execution was observed; the faulting access is a near-NULL read that is not exploitable for control-flow hijacking.

Fix / mitigation status:
The issue was fixed in GPAC commit:
```
e6d01820d7bf3967d931fedb379ee5f209bc133b
```
Users should update to a GPAC build containing this commit or later.

References:

- Issue: https://github.com/gpac/gpac/issues/3321
- PoC: https://github.com/sigdevel/pocs/blob/main/res/gpac/MP4Box/57/57_dump_isom_saps_filedump_c_1743
- Fix: https://github.com/gpac/gpac/commit/e6d01820d7bf3967d931fedb379ee5f209bc133b

Credit
Alexander A. Shvedov (@sigdevel)

Security Advisory: CVE-2025-60487 - NULL Pointer Dereference in GPAC/MP4Box

Processing an empty XML file with the `-dnal` option triggers a NULL pointer dereference in `gf_isom_get_esd`, causing MP4Box to crash.

Summary:
The `gf_isom_get_esd` function in `isomedia/isom_read.c` retrieves an Elementary Stream Descriptor for a given track. When processing an empty file with an `.xml` extension, the ISO media layer lacks a valid media descriptor structure; the function dereferences a near-NULL pointer at address 0x50 (80-byte struct offset) without validating the pointer beforehand, terminating the process with SIGSEGV.

CWE:
CWE-476 - NULL Pointer Dereference

Affected Component:
```
isomedia/isom_read.c:1386
Function: gf_isom_get_esd()
```

Affected Product:
MP4Box (GPAC Multimedia Open Source Project)

Affected Version:
2.5-DEV-rev1665-g3f20eb0cd-master; commit `3f20eb0cd22116367c036e6ffe6ace299b38d686`. Any codebase equivalent to this commit that has not applied the fix commit is affected.

Attack Conditions:
An attacker supplies an empty or minimal file with an `.xml` extension. Local access is required; the victim must invoke `MP4Box -dnal 1 <crafted_file>.xml` or any equivalent NAL dump command that triggers the ESD retrieval path on an uninitialized media context.

Impact:
The near-NULL pointer dereference (READ at address 0x000000000050) causes an immediate process crash, resulting in Denial of Service. No evidence of arbitrary code execution was observed; the faulting access is a near-NULL read that is not exploitable for control-flow hijacking.

Fix / mitigation status:
The issue was fixed in GPAC commit:
```
e6d01820d7bf3967d931fedb379ee5f209bc133b
```
Users should update to a GPAC build containing this commit or later.

References:

- Issue: https://github.com/gpac/gpac/issues/3320
- PoC: N/A (see issue)
- Fix: https://github.com/gpac/gpac/commit/e6d01820d7bf3967d931fedb379ee5f209bc133b

CVE-2025-60490

sigdevel at 2026-10-17T14:03:12.962Z ##

Security Advisory: CVE-2025-60490 - Use-After-Free in GPAC/MP4Box

Processing a crafted MPEG-2 Transport Stream file triggers a heap use-after-free in `gf_filter_pid_send_event_downstream` when a freed PID instance is accessed during event propagation.

Summary:
When parsing a crafted MPEG-2 TS file with malformed PMT descriptors and sync marker violations, `gf_filter_pid_inst_swap_delete` frees a PID instance at `filter_pid.c:551`. A stale reference to this freed 344-byte region is subsequently read in `gf_filter_pid_send_event_downstream` at line 7919 (READ of 8 bytes at offset 0 of the freed object) because the pointer is not cleared after deallocation.

CWE:
CWE-416 - Use After Free

Affected Component:
```
filter_core/filter_pid.c:7919
Function: gf_filter_pid_send_event_downstream()
```

Affected Product:
MP4Box (GPAC Multimedia Open Source Project)

Affected Version:
2.5-DEV-rev1665-g3f20eb0cd-master; commit `3f20eb0cd22116367c036e6ffe6ace299b38d686`. Any codebase equivalent to this commit that has not applied the fix commit is affected.

Attack Conditions:
An attacker supplies a crafted MPEG-2 TS file containing broken PMT descriptor sizes, unsupported stream types, and missing sync markers. Local access is required; the victim must invoke `MP4Box -add <crafted_file> -new /dev/null` or any equivalent import command that triggers the filter PID event propagation path.

Impact:
The use-after-free (READ of size 8 at offset 0 inside a freed 344-byte heap region) results in process termination, causing Denial of Service. Arbitrary code execution is possible; the first MITRE request explicitly marks code execution impact as true, and use-after-free vulnerabilities at the start of a controlled heap object are a known code-execution primitive.

Fix / mitigation status:
The issue was fixed in GPAC commit:
```
d0793f92e04fe284a25c2a9335cc5edc68deb6d4
```
Users should update to a GPAC build containing this commit or later.

References:

- Issue: https://github.com/gpac/gpac/issues/3315
- PoC: https://github.com/sigdevel/pocs/blob/main/res/gpac/MP4Box/54/54_gf_filter_pid_send_event_downstream_filter_core_filter_pid_c_7919
- Fix: https://github.com/gpac/gpac/commit/d0793f92e04fe284a25c2a9335cc5edc68deb6d4

Credit
Alexander A. Shvedov (@sigdevel)

Security Advisory: CVE-2025-60490 - Use-After-Free in GPAC/MP4Box

Processing a crafted MPEG-2 Transport Stream file triggers a heap use-after-free in `gf_filter_pid_send_event_downstream` when a freed PID instance is accessed during event propagation.

Summary:
When parsing a crafted MPEG-2 TS file with malformed PMT descriptors and sync marker violations, `gf_filter_pid_inst_swap_delete` frees a PID instance at `filter_pid.c:551`. A stale reference to this freed 344-byte region is subsequently read in `gf_filter_pid_send_event_downstream` at line 7919 (READ of 8 bytes at offset 0 of the freed object) because the pointer is not cleared after deallocation.

CWE:
CWE-416 - Use After Free

Affected Component:
```
filter_core/filter_pid.c:7919
Function: gf_filter_pid_send_event_downstream()
```

Affected Product:
MP4Box (GPAC Multimedia Open Source Project)

Affected Version:
2.5-DEV-rev1665-g3f20eb0cd-master; commit `3f20eb0cd22116367c036e6ffe6ace299b38d686`. Any codebase equivalent to this commit that has not applied the fix commit is affected.

Attack Conditions:
An attacker supplies a crafted MPEG-2 TS file containing broken PMT descriptor sizes, unsupported stream types, and missing sync markers. Local access is required; the victim must invoke `MP4Box -add <crafted_file> -new /dev/null` or any equivalent import command that triggers the filter PID event propagation path.

Impact:
The use-after-free (READ of size 8 at offset 0 inside a freed 344-byte heap region) results in process termination, causing Denial of Service. Arbitrary code execution is possible; the first MITRE request explicitly marks code execution impact as true, and use-after-free vulnerabilities at the start of a controlled heap object are a known code-execution primitive.

Fix / mitigation status:
The issue was fixed in GPAC commit:
```
d0793f92e04fe284a25c2a9335cc5edc68deb6d4
```
Users should update to a GPAC build containing this commit or later.

References:

Security Advisory: CVE-2025-60478 - Heap Use-After-Free in GPAC Packet Destruction

Processing a crafted MPEG-2 TS input with MP4Box DASH segmentation can trigger a heap use-after-free in `gf_filter_packet_destroy()`.

Summary:
Malformed program table data and empty DASH segment state can cause GPAC to mishandle packet ownership. The file output and PID packet drop path later destroy a packet object that has already been invalidated.

AddressSanitizer reports `heap-use-after-free` in `filter_core/filter_pck.c`.

CWE:
CWE-416 - Use After Free

Affected Component:
```
filter_core/filter_pck.c:678
Function: gf_filter_packet_destroy()
```

Relevant ASAN stack:

```
gf_filter_packet_destroy filter_core/filter_pck.c:678
gf_filter_packet_destroy filter_core/filter_pck.c:684
gf_filter_pid_drop_packet filter_core/filter_pid.c:7270
fileout_process filters/out_file.c:933
```

Affected Product:
MP4Box (GPAC Multimedia Open Source Project)

Affected Version:
```
GPAC version: 2.5-DEV-rev1617-g856674b22-master
Commit: 856674b226d6cbe28a941ad223be38194cbf7d37
```

Attack Conditions:
An attacker supplies a crafted MPEG-2 TS file that is processed by MP4Box DASH segmentation:

```
./MP4Box -dash 100 47_gf_filter_packet_destroy_filter_core_filter_pck_c_678
```

Impact:
The observed impact is Denial of Service. Because the issue is a heap use-after-free, memory corruption risk is also present.

Fix / mitigation status:
The MITRE response in the local data records the fix as:

```
c592a144bbe339ee581ca88aaa3247362795b742
```

References:

- CVE: https://www.cve.org/CVERecord?id=CVE-2025-60478
- Issue: https://github.com/gpac/gpac/issues/3300
- PoC: https://github.com/sigdevel/pocs/blob/main/res/gpac/MP4Box/47/47_gf_filter_packet_destroy_filter_core_filter_pck_c_678
- Fix: https://github.com/gpac/gpac/commit/c592a144bbe339ee581ca88aaa3247362795b742

Alexander A. Shvedov (@sigdevel)

Security Advisory: CVE-2025-60482 - Use-After-Free in GPAC MP4Box AC-4 Descriptor Cleanup

Processing a crafted AC-4 stream with MP4Box can trigger a heap use-after-free in `gf_odf_ac4_cfg_clean_list()`.

Summary:
The recursive AC-4 descriptor cleanup path can revisit or count a list object that has already been freed. When MP4Box imports a crafted AC-4 input, descriptor destruction reaches `gf_odf_ac4_cfg_clean_list()` and then `gf_list_count()` on stale heap memory.

AddressSanitizer reports `heap-use-after-free`.

CWE:
CWE-416 - Use After Free

Affected Component:
```
odf/descriptors.c:2411
Function: gf_odf_ac4_cfg_clean_list()

utils/list.c:664
Function: gf_list_count()
```

Relevant ASAN stack:

```
gf_list_count utils/list.c:664
gf_odf_ac4_cfg_clean_list odf/descriptors.c:2411
gf_odf_ac4_cfg_clean_list odf/descriptors.c:2400
dac4_box_del isomedia/box_code_base.c:9218
```

Affected Product:
MP4Box (GPAC Multimedia Open Source Project)

Affected Version:
```
GPAC version: 2.5-DEV-rev1593-gfe88c3545-master
Commit: fe88c3545aadd597b250ccf23271d5d3de50ccc8
```

Attack Conditions:
An attacker supplies a crafted AC-4 file that is imported by MP4Box:

```
./MP4Box -add 46_gf_odf_ac4_cfg_clean_list_odf_descriptors_c_2411 -new /dev/null
```

Impact:
The observed impact is Denial of Service. Because the bug is a heap use-after-free, memory corruption risk is also present.

Fix / mitigation status:
The MITRE response in the local data records the fix as:

```
e02d1fd24cdc26acb1b236ab38b3832cffcae21b
```

References:

- CVE: https://www.cve.org/CVERecord?id=CVE-2025-60482
- Issue: https://github.com/gpac/gpac/issues/3297
- PoC: https://github.com/sigdevel/pocs/blob/main/res/gpac/MP4Box/46/46_gf_odf_ac4_cfg_clean_list_odf_descriptors_c_2411
- Fix: https://github.com/gpac/gpac/commit/e02d1fd24cdc26acb1b236ab38b3832cffcae21b

Security Advisory: CVE-2025-60480 - Heap Buffer Overflow in GPAC MP4Box AC-4 Alternative Info Parsing

A heap buffer overflow vulnerability exists in GPAC MP4Box when processing a crafted AC-4 stream. The issue occurs in `gf_odf_ac4_cfg_alternative_info()` in `odf/descriptors.c` and can be triggered by running MP4Box with the `-info` option on a malformed input file.

Summary:
AddressSanitizer confirms a heap-buffer-overflow at `odf/descriptors.c:1899`. The vulnerable code reads 1 byte past the end of an allocated buffer due to insufficient bounds checking of AC-4 `alt_group_id` array indexes.

The crafted AC-4 stream reaches AC-4 descriptor generation and causes `gf_odf_ac4_cfg_alternative_info()` to access `alt_group_id` data outside the bounds of an allocated buffer. The allocation originates while parsing the raw AC-4 frame, and the out-of-bounds read occurs later while writing AC-4 descriptor information.

Relevant AddressSanitizer trace:

```
==1847116==ERROR: AddressSanitizer: heap-buffer-overflow on address 0x518000000f90
READ of size 1 at 0x518000000f90 thread T0
#0 0x... in gf_odf_ac4_cfg_alternative_info odf/descriptors.c:1899
#1 0x... in gf_odf_ac4_cfg_presentation_v1_dsi odf/descriptors.c:2103
#2 0x... in gf_odf_ac4_cfg_dsi_v1 odf/descriptors.c:2233
#3 0x... in gf_odf_ac4_cfg_write_bs odf/descriptors.c:2281
#4 0x... in gf_odf_ac4_cfg_write odf/descriptors.c:2288
#5 0x... in ac4dmx_check_pid filters/reframe_ac4.c:238
#6 0x... in ac4dmx_process filters/reframe_ac4.c:403
#7 0x... in gf_filter_process_task filter_core/filter.c:3187
#8 0x... in gf_fs_thread_proc filter_core/filter_session.c:2421
#9 0x... in gf_fs_run filter_core/filter_session.c:2728
#10 0x... in gf_media_import media_tools/media_import.c:1278
#11 0x... in convert_file_info applications/mp4box/fileimport.c:131
#12 0x... in mp4box_main applications/mp4box/mp4box.c:6520

0x518000000f90 is located 0 bytes after 784-byte region [0x518000000c80,0x518000000f90)
allocated by thread T0 here:
#0 0x... in malloc
#1 0x... in gf_ac4_raw_frame media_tools/av_parsers.c:15916
#2 0x... in gf_ac4_parser_bs media_tools/av_parsers.c:16113

SUMMARY: AddressSanitizer: heap-buffer-overflow odf/descriptors.c:1899 in gf_odf_ac4_cfg_alternative_info
```

CWE:
CWE-122 - Heap-based Buffer Overflow

Affected Component:
```
odf/descriptors.c:1899
Function: gf_odf_ac4_cfg_alternative_info()
```

Affected Product:
MP4Box (GPAC Multimedia Open Source Project)

Affected Version:
```
2.5-DEV-rev1593-gfe88c3545-master
Commit: fe88c3545aadd597b250ccf23271d5d3de50ccc8
```

Attack Conditions:
An attacker supplies a crafted input file that is processed by MP4Box. The issue can be reproduced locally with:
```
./MP4Box -info 44_gf_odf_ac4_cfg_alternative_info_odf_descriptors_c_1899
```

Attack vector: local crafted AC-4 file processed by MP4Box
User interaction: required

Impact:
denial of service via application crash; local MITRE data also identifies potential arbitrary code execution risk

Fix / mitigation status:
The issue was fixed in GPAC commit:
```
e02d1fd24cdc26acb1b236ab38b3832cffcae21b
```
Users should update to a GPAC build containing this commit or later.

References:

- Issue: https://github.com/gpac/gpac/issues/3295

sigdevel at 2026-10-17T13:27:56.312Z ##

Security Advisory: CVE-2025-60470 - Out-of-Bounds Read in GPAC MP4Box AC-4 Parser

An out-of-bounds read vulnerability exists in GPAC MP4Box when processing a crafted AC-4 stream. The issue occurs in `gf_ac4_parser_bs()` in `media_tools/av_parsers.c` and is reachable during media import through MP4Box commands such as `-info`.

Summary:
AddressSanitizer confirms a global-buffer-overflow at `media_tools/av_parsers.c:16137`. The parser reads 4 bytes past the end of the global `AC4_SAMPLE_DELTA_TABLE_441` array due to insufficient bounds checking of AC-4 sample delta table indexes.

The crafted AC-4 stream causes the AC-4 parser to access `AC4_SAMPLE_DELTA_TABLE_441` with an out-of-bounds index. The invalid read occurs while probing the AC-4 stream and then propagates through the MP4Box media import path.

Relevant AddressSanitizer trace:

```
==1845261==ERROR: AddressSanitizer: global-buffer-overflow on address 0x7f4214ddf8fc
READ of size 4 at 0x7f4214ddf8fc thread T0
#0 0x... in gf_ac4_parser_bs media_tools/av_parsers.c:16137
#1 0x... in ac4dmx_probe_data filters/reframe_ac4.c:546
#2 0x... in gf_filter_pid_raw_new filter_core/filter.c:4704
#3 0x... in filein_process filters/in_file.c:610
#4 0x... in gf_filter_process_task filter_core/filter.c:3187
#5 0x... in gf_fs_thread_proc filter_core/filter_session.c:2421
#6 0x... in gf_fs_run filter_core/filter_session.c:2728
#7 0x... in gf_media_import media_tools/media_import.c:1278
#8 0x... in convert_file_info applications/mp4box/fileimport.c:131
#9 0x... in mp4box_main applications/mp4box/mp4box.c:6520

0x7f4214ddf8fc is located 36 bytes before global variable 'AC4_SAMPLE_DELTA_TABLE_48'
```

Security Advisory: CVE-2025-60470 - Out-of-Bounds Read in GPAC MP4Box AC-4 Parser

An out-of-bounds read vulnerability exists in GPAC MP4Box when processing a crafted AC-4 stream. The issue occurs in `gf_ac4_parser_bs()` in `media_tools/av_parsers.c` and is reachable during media import through MP4Box commands such as `-info`.

Summary:
AddressSanitizer confirms a global-buffer-overflow at `media_tools/av_parsers.c:16137`. The parser reads 4 bytes past the end of the global `AC4_SAMPLE_DELTA_TABLE_441` array due to insufficient bounds checking of AC-4 sample delta table indexes.

The crafted AC-4 stream causes the AC-4 parser to access `AC4_SAMPLE_DELTA_TABLE_441` with an out-of-bounds index. The invalid read occurs while probing the AC-4 stream and then propagates through the MP4Box media import path.

Relevant AddressSanitizer trace:


Security Advisory: CVE-2025-60476 - Heap Use-After-Free in GPAC MP4Box AC-4 Descriptor Cleanup

A heap use-after-free vulnerability exists in GPAC MP4Box when processing a crafted AC-4 file. The issue occurs in `gf_odf_ac4_cfg_clean_list()` in `odf/descriptors.c` during recursive cleanup of AC-4 configuration lists.

Summary:
AddressSanitizer confirms a heap-use-after-free at `odf/descriptors.c:2419`. The cleanup code accesses a list node after it has already been freed in a recursive cleanup path, causing MP4Box to crash.

The crafted AC-4 stream contains malformed configuration list data. During parsing and cleanup, `gf_odf_ac4_cfg_clean_list()` recursively destroys AC-4 configuration structures. A node is freed in a deeper cleanup level and later accessed again by the caller, resulting in a read from freed heap memory.

Relevant AddressSanitizer trace:

```
[AC4] Don't support presentation_version 0.
==1841011==ERROR: AddressSanitizer: heap-use-after-free on address 0x508000000470
READ of size 8 at 0x508000000470 thread T0
#0 0x... in gf_odf_ac4_cfg_clean_list odf/descriptors.c:2419
#1 0x... in gf_odf_ac4_cfg_clean_list odf/descriptors.c:2400
#2 0x... in gf_ac4_parser_bs media_tools/av_parsers.c:16101
#3 0x... in ac4dmx_process filters/reframe_ac4.c:388

#9 0x... in mp4box_main applications/mp4box/mp4box.c:6520

freed by thread T0 here:
#0 0x... in free
#1 0x... in gf_odf_ac4_cfg_clean_list odf/descriptors.c:2432
#2 0x... in gf_odf_ac4_cfg_clean_list odf/descriptors.c:2400

previously allocated by thread T0 here:
#0 0x... in malloc
#1 0x... in gf_ac4_raw_frame media_tools/av_parsers.c:15938
#2 0x... in gf_ac4_parser_bs media_tools/av_parsers.c:16113

SUMMARY: AddressSanitizer: heap-use-after-free odf/descriptors.c:2419 in gf_odf_ac4_cfg_clean_list
```

CWE-416 - Use After Free

Security Advisory: CVE-2025-60469 - NULL Pointer Dereference in GPAC MP4Box AC-4 Parser

A NULL pointer dereference vulnerability exists in GPAC MP4Box when processing a crafted AC-4 stream. The issue occurs in `gf_ac4_presentation_ch_mode()` in `media_tools/av_parsers.c` and can be triggered by running MP4Box with the `-info` option on a malformed input file.

Summary:
AddressSanitizer confirms a segmentation fault at `media_tools/av_parsers.c:15641`. The parser attempts to read through a NULL or invalid pointer while handling an AC-4 stream with unsupported `presentation_version` and missing substream group information.

The crafted AC-4 stream declares an unsupported presentation version and omits required substream group data. MP4Box reports that `presentation_version 0` is unsupported and that substream group `1` cannot be found for presentation `0`. The error path then reaches `gf_ac4_presentation_ch_mode()` and dereferences an invalid pointer, resulting in a segmentation fault.

Relevant AddressSanitizer trace:

```
[AC4] Don't support presentation_version 0.
[AC4] Cannot find substream group 1 for presentation 0
AddressSanitizer:DEADLYSIGNAL
==1829578==ERROR: AddressSanitizer: SEGV on unknown address 0x000000000048
==1829578==The signal is caused by a READ memory access.
==1829578==Hint: address points to the zero page.
```

sigdevel at 2026-10-17T12:55:39.258Z ##

Security Advisory: CVE-2025-60475 - Global Buffer Overflow in GPAC MP4Box AC-4 Parser

A global buffer overflow vulnerability exists in GPAC MP4Box when processing a crafted AC-4 audio stream. The issue occurs in `gf_ac4_parser_bs()` in `media_tools/av_parsers.c` and can be triggered by running MP4Box with the `-info` option on a malformed input file.

Summary:
AddressSanitizer confirms an out-of-bounds read at `media_tools/av_parsers.c:16140`. The parser accesses `AC4_SAMPLE_DELTA_TABLE_48` with an out-of-bounds index, causing a 4-byte read past the global array boundary.
The AC-4 parser reads index values from the input bitstream and uses them to access the global `AC4_SAMPLE_DELTA_TABLE_48` array. The vulnerable path does not properly validate the index before accessing the table. A crafted AC-4 stream can therefore cause a read just past the end of the global array.

CWE:
CWE-125 - Out-of-bounds Read

Affected Component:
```
media_tools/av_parsers.c:16140
Function: gf_ac4_parser_bs()
```

Affected Product:
MP4Box (GPAC Multimedia Open Source Project)

Affected Version:
```
2.5-DEV-rev1593-gfe88c3545-master
Commit: fe88c3545aadd597b250ccf23271d5d3de50ccc8
```

Attack Conditions:
An attacker supplies a crafted input file that is processed by MP4Box. The issue can be reproduced locally with:
```
./MP4Box -info 40_gf_ac4_parser_bs_media_tools_av_parsers_c_16140
```

Attack vector: local crafted AC-4 file processed by MP4Box
User interaction: required

Impact:
denial of service via application crash; local triage notes also identify potential information disclosure or code execution risk

Fix / mitigation status:
The issue was fixed in GPAC commit:
```
e02d1fd24cdc26acb1b236ab38b3832cffcae21b
```
Users should update to a GPAC build containing this commit or later.

References:

- Issue: https://github.com/gpac/gpac/issues/3291
- Fix: https://github.com/gpac/gpac/commit/e02d1fd24cdc26acb1b236ab38b3832cffcae21b

##
sigdevel@infosec.exchange at 2026-10-17T12:55:39.000Z ##

Security Advisory: CVE-2025-60475 - Global Buffer Overflow in GPAC MP4Box AC-4 Parser

A global buffer overflow vulnerability exists in GPAC MP4Box when processing a crafted AC-4 audio stream. The issue occurs in `gf_ac4_parser_bs()` in `media_tools/av_parsers.c` and can be triggered by running MP4Box with the `-info` option on a malformed input file.

Summary:
AddressSanitizer confirms an out-of-bounds read at `media_tools/av_parsers.c:16140`. The parser accesses `AC4_SAMPLE_DELTA_TABLE_48` with an out-of-bounds index, causing a 4-byte read past the global array boundary.
The AC-4 parser reads index values from the input bitstream and uses them to access the global `AC4_SAMPLE_DELTA_TABLE_48` array. The vulnerable path does not properly validate the index before accessing the table. A crafted AC-4 stream can therefore cause a read just past the end of the global array.

CWE:
CWE-125 - Out-of-bounds Read

Affected Component:
```
media_tools/av_parsers.c:16140
Function: gf_ac4_parser_bs()
```

Affected Product:
MP4Box (GPAC Multimedia Open Source Project)

Affected Version:
```
2.5-DEV-rev1593-gfe88c3545-master
Commit: fe88c3545aadd597b250ccf23271d5d3de50ccc8
```

Attack Conditions:
An attacker supplies a crafted input file that is processed by MP4Box. The issue can be reproduced locally with:
```
./MP4Box -info 40_gf_ac4_parser_bs_media_tools_av_parsers_c_16140
```

Attack vector: local crafted AC-4 file processed by MP4Box
User interaction: required

Impact:
denial of service via application crash; local triage notes also identify potential information disclosure or code execution risk

Fix / mitigation status:
The issue was fixed in GPAC commit:
```
e02d1fd24cdc26acb1b236ab38b3832cffcae21b
```
Users should update to a GPAC build containing this commit or later.

References:

- Issue: https://github.com/gpac/gpac/issues/3291
- Fix: https://github.com/gpac/gpac/commit/e02d1fd24cdc26acb1b236ab38b3832cffcae21b
- PoC: https://github.com/sigdevel/pocs/blob/main/res/gpac/MP4Box/40/40_gf_ac4_parser_bs_media_tools_av_parsers_c_16140

Security Advisory: CVE-2025-60479 - NULL Pointer Dereference in GPAC/MP4Box

Processing a crafted MPEG-4 file with broken BIFS vector fields triggers a NULL pointer dereference in `StatSFVec2f`, causing MP4Box to crash during scene statistics collection.

Summary:
The `StatSFVec2f` function in `scene_manager/scene_stats.c` collects statistics for SFVec2f fields in an MPEG-4 BIFS scene. When the scene contains a crafted broken vector field, the statistics traversal passes a NULL pointer to `StatSFVec2f` without validation. The function dereferences the pointer at address 0x0, terminating the process with SIGSEGV.

CWE:
CWE-476 - NULL Pointer Dereference

Affected Component:
```
scene_manager/scene_stats.c:192
Function: StatSFVec2f()
```

Affected Product:
MP4Box (GPAC Multimedia Open Source Project)

Affected Version:
2.5-DEV-rev1678-gb7ffd9f96-master; commit `b7ffd9f96bc188dde148300d8c4e510b557563aa`. Any codebase equivalent to this commit that has not applied the fix commit is affected.

Attack Conditions:
An attacker supplies a crafted MPEG-4 file containing a malformed BIFS scene with invalid SFVec2f vector field structures. Local access is required; the victim must invoke `MP4Box -nstat <crafted_file>` or any equivalent scene statistics command that triggers the scene analysis path.

Impact:
The NULL pointer dereference (READ at address 0x000000000000) causes an immediate process crash, resulting in Denial of Service. No evidence of arbitrary code execution was observed; the faulting access is a NULL read that is not exploitable for control-flow hijacking.

Fix / mitigation status:
The issue was fixed in GPAC commit:
```
75786eb61b7cc838b57c3d4f8004b4f13b5b724c
```
Users should update to a GPAC build containing this commit or later.

References:

- Issue: https://github.com/gpac/gpac/issues/3322
- PoC: https://github.com/sigdevel/pocs/blob/main/res/gpac/MP4Box/58/58_StatSFVec2f_scene_manager_scene_stats_c_192

Security Advisory: CVE-2025-60489 - NULL Pointer Dereference in GPAC/MP4Box

Processing a crafted MP4 file with corrupted DTS timing metadata triggers a NULL pointer dereference in `dasher_insert_timeline_entry`, causing MP4Box to crash during DASH timeline construction.

Summary:
The `dasher_insert_timeline_entry` function in `filters/dasher.c` accesses timeline entry structures during DASH segment processing. When the input MP4 has corrupted DTS sequence data that produces empty DASH segments, the dasher fails to validate a timeline entry pointer before dereferencing it at address 0xc (12-byte struct offset), leading to a near-NULL read and SIGSEGV.

Security Advisory: CVE-2025-60491 - Use-After-Free in GPAC/MP4Box

Processing a crafted MPEG-2 TS file with corrupted PMT descriptors triggers a heap use-after-free in `dasher_flush_segment`, causing MP4Box to crash during DASH segmentation.

Summary:
The `dasher_configure_pid` function in `filters/dasher.c` frees a PID context structure at line 986 when reconfiguring a stream from a crafted MPEG-TS input. The freed pointer is not nullified, and `dasher_flush_segment` subsequently reads 4 bytes at offset 336 within that freed 1096-byte region at line 8062 during the segment flush cycle, constituting a heap-use-after-free.

CWE:
CWE-416 - Use After Free

Affected Component:
```
filters/dasher.c:8062
Function: dasher_flush_segment()
```

Affected Product:
MP4Box (GPAC Multimedia Open Source Project)

Affected Version:
2.5-DEV-rev1698-gfc2009fa7-master; commit `fc2009fa75631960743f8f7855dda8998167b5ab`. Any codebase equivalent to this commit that has not applied the fix commit is affected.

Attack Conditions:
An attacker supplies a crafted MPEG-2 TS file containing broken PMT descriptor sizes, reused PIDs, and sync marker violations. Local access is required; the victim must invoke `MP4Box -dash 1000 <crafted_file>` or any equivalent DASH segmentation command that triggers PID reconfiguration in the dasher module.

Impact:
The use-after-free (READ of size 4 at 336 bytes into a freed 1096-byte heap region) causes process termination, resulting in Denial of Service. Code execution cannot be ruled out; use-after-free vulnerabilities allow an attacker to control freed memory contents and potentially redirect execution flow.

Fix / mitigation status:
The issue was fixed in GPAC commit:
```
f6128214feb69cd689e4b9944a2621bc4ad8faf0
```
Users should update to a GPAC build containing this commit or later.

References:

- Issue: https://github.com/gpac/gpac/issues/3326
- PoC: https://github.com/sigdevel/pocs/blob/main/res/gpac/MP4Box/61/61_dasher_flush_segment_filters_dasher_c_8062
- Fix: https://github.com/gpac/gpac/commit/f6128214feb69cd689e4b9944a2621bc4ad8faf0

Security Advisory: CVE-2025-60496 - Divide by Zero in GPAC/MP4Box

Splitting a truncated MP4 file with uninitialized stream parameters triggers a divide-by-zero in `reframer_send_packet`, causing MP4Box to crash with SIGFPE.

Summary:
The `reframer_send_packet` function in `filters/reframer.c` computes split boundaries using stream parameters such as timescale and estimated file size. When upstream parsing of a truncated MP4 leaves these values uninitialized (zero), the arithmetic at line 1026 performs a division by zero, raising SIGFPE and terminating the process immediately.

CWE:
CWE-369 - Divide By Zero

Affected Component:
```
filters/reframer.c:1026
Function: reframer_send_packet()
```

Affected Product:
MP4Box (GPAC Multimedia Open Source Project)

Affected Version:
2.5-DEV-rev1780-g50b5741f2-master; commit `50b5741f291126b610c59db433fc02e8a17f0c5d`. Any codebase equivalent to this commit that has not applied the fix commit is affected.

Attack Conditions:
An attacker supplies a truncated MP4 file that causes the ISO media parser to leave stream parameters (timescale, file size estimate, or sample rate) at zero. Local access is required; the victim must invoke `MP4Box -split-size 8000 <crafted_file>` or any equivalent split command that triggers the reframer size-estimation path.

Impact:
The divide-by-zero (SIGFPE) causes an immediate process crash, resulting in Denial of Service. No evidence of arbitrary code execution was observed; floating-point exceptions of this type are not exploitable for control-flow hijacking.

Fix / mitigation status:
The issue was fixed in GPAC commit:
```
f03c946aae129980f0426d4cbae9746d0536fee6
```
Users should update to a GPAC build containing this commit or later.

References:

- Issue: https://github.com/gpac/gpac/issues/3334
- PoC: https://github.com/sigdevel/pocs/blob/main/res/gpac/MP4Box/65/65_reframer_send_packet_filters_reframer_c_1026
- Fix: https://github.com/gpac/gpac/commit/f03c946aae129980f0426d4cbae9746d0536fee6

A TP-Link EAP770 v1 owner says the access point corrupts large Wi-Fi downloads and that TP-Link support told him v1 is end of life. He blames Qualcomm's qca-nss-dp offload driver; the code he cites does skip cache invalidation on recycled receive buffers. Locked out of root, he says he chained CVE-2022-0185 (public January 2022) on the AP's Linux 5.4.164 kernel to disable the offload, at about 8% lower speed.
